# R1 deviation audit for v0.1.0

Audit date: 2026-09-21. Reference revisions: glam-rs `0.33.8` (`465b600`) and glamx
`v0.3.1` (`f530eef`). This is a read-only audit of the library: no Cairo source was changed.

## Executive summary

The extractor found 1,179 public items with a `#### Deviations` section: 503 say `None.` and 676
describe at least one difference. The fixed-point and fused-kernel differences are generally
deliberate and well documented, but the surface is not ready to freeze unchanged. Four findings
are release blockers:

1. `Rot2::lerp` normalizes, unlike glamx, and therefore implements nlerp under the upstream name.
2. `Rot2::is_normalized` is missing without being listed among the omitted APIs.
3. `camera.cairo` says eight Affine3/Quat view constructors are not ported although they exist.
4. Panic coverage cannot meet the stated one-test-per-path rule: 839 documented literal-message
   occurrences are represented by only 634 `#[should_panic(expected: ...)]` tests, before removing
   test-helper panics.

The threshold families are internally consistent except for decomposition of degenerate affine
transforms: `Affine2::to_scale_angle_translation` leaves the upstream nonzero determinant/scale
precondition unchecked and can return a zero scale, whereas the 3D path reaches a mandatory
division and panics. This difference is documented on the item but is not a DESIGN row.

## Method

`scripts/deviations.py` recursively reads the committed Cairo sources under
`packages/{fixed,glam,glamx}/src`, associates each template doc block with its declaration, and
emits file, declaration line, owner, kind, item, `Mirrors`, `Panics`, and `Deviations`. Default
output is TSV; `--format markdown` produces the appendix; `--check` byte-compares the appendix to
fresh output. Nested generated camera and swizzle modules are included. `lib.cairo` files are not,
because they contain re-exports rather than documented items.

Classification is by primary semantic difference per item. When an item has multiple bullets,
the specific DESIGN section 3 row wins over the general fixed-rounding class; genuinely separate
secondary differences are called out below. Consequently these counts describe items, not bullet
frequency.

| Primary class | Items |
|---|---:|
| `None.` | 503 |
| scalar rounding and fused evaluation (DESIGN section 2) | 169 |
| not covered by DESIGN | 130 |
| `glam_assert!` | 80 |
| scalar operators | 76 |
| overflow | 55 |
| NaN / infinity | 38 |
| epsilon thresholds | 22 |
| division by zero | 20 |
| casts | 20 |
| `signum(0)` | 18 |
| `round` / `fract` / remainder | 17 |
| inverse | 16 |
| `abs_diff_eq`, exact equality, hash | 9 |
| normalize of zero | 4 |
| `acos_approx` | 2 |
| **Total** | **1,179** |

The 130-item last class is mostly repeated sibling-type instances. The distinct semantic families
are enumerated next; performance notes and statements such as “Exact” are included because they
currently occupy `Deviations` bullets even when they are not behavioral deviations.

## Deviations not covered by DESIGN

| Distinct deviation | Items carrying it | Upstream and port evidence | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Rot2::lerp` is normalized | `Rot2::lerp` | glamx `src/rot2.rs:262-266` returns the component-wise linear combination. Cairo normalizes it at `packages/glamx/src/rot2.cairo:378-385`. | **align with upstream** |
| Missing `Rot2::is_normalized` | `Rot2` surface | Upstream defines it at glamx `src/rot2.rs:145-149`; Cairo has no declaration and the type-level omissions at `packages/glamx/src/rot2.cairo:29-33` do not mention it. | **bug** |
| Other explicit API omissions | Type-level sections for vectors, matrices, booleans, Rot2; `from_matrix_unchecked`; `col_mut`; `set_row`; iterator/reference overloads | Upstream exposes these; Cairo omits them for language/scope reasons. Most are justified by DESIGN sections 1/4, but individual omissions are not centrally enumerated. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| API additions and compatibility aliases | `SdpMatrix{2,3}::identity`, subtraction impls, `SymmetricEigen3::from_sdp`, `quat_from_affine3`, checked inverse variants | These names do not exist upstream; their item docs identify the addition. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Linear interpolation formula | `Fixed::lerp`, `Vec{2,3,4}::lerp`, Pose translations | glam-rs uses `a*(1-s)+b*s` (`src/f32/vec3.rs:974-975`); Cairo uses `a+(b-a)*s`, exact at endpoints and with one rescale. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| `move_towards` removes the `1e-4` snap band | `Vec{2,3,4}::move_towards` | glam-rs returns `rhs` for `len <= 1e-4` (`src/f32/vec3.rs:984-990`); Cairo only treats raw zero specially (`packages/glam/src/vec3.cairo:1137`, implementation at `:2017-2027`). | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Reversed clamp range has a different out-of-contract result | `Fixed::clamp`, all float/integer vector `clamp` methods | Rust scalar clamp panics; glam vectors compute `self.max(min).min(max)` after an optional assertion. Cairo's branch order can instead select `min` (`packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:219-227`; `packages/glam/src/vec3.cairo:269-277`). | **document better** |
| Checked camera preconditions | all 26 projection wrappers | Upstream checks only positive near/far behind `glam_assert!` (`src/camera/camera_impl.rs:130`, `:174`, `:204`). Cairo always checks near/far and additionally checks `0 < fov < PI` and nonzero aspect (`packages/glam/src/camera/camera_impl.cairo:54-79`). | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Cyclic-Jacobi eigen solver replaces the closed form | `SymmetricEigen3::{new,eigenvalues}`, `Mat3Ext::symmetric_eigen*` | glamx uses cubic roots and a `d_max < 1e-20` eigenvector fallback (`src/eigen3.rs:120-154`). Cairo uses scaled Jacobi sweeps and Rayleigh refinement (`packages/glamx/src/eigen3.cairo:8-63`). | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Affine decomposition degeneracy is inconsistent | `Affine2::to_scale_angle_translation`; `Affine3`/`Mat4::to_scale_rotation_translation` | glam-rs asserts nonzero determinant and every scale (`src/f32/affine2.rs:253-260`; `src/f32/scalar/mat4.rs:303-317`). Cairo 2D returns zero scale (`packages/glam/src/affine2.cairo:325-336`), while 3D divides by each scale and panics (`packages/glam/src/mat4.cairo:1464-1485`). | **bug** |
| Algebraic reassociation beyond the scalar rule | fused dot/cross/matrix/transform/rejection/midpoint methods across `glam` and `glamx` | Upstream rounds each f32 operation; Cairo accumulates products wide and rescales once. DESIGN section 2 requires this but does not state explicitly that reassociation changes results relative to a line-by-line port. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Integer intermediate overflow differs by build mode | arithmetic, element sums/products, Manhattan distance in `IVec*`/`UVec*` | glam-rs native integer expressions panic in debug and wrap in release; Cairo checks only the fused final result in several methods and always panics for plain operators. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Exact fixed algorithms replace approximations | trig/exp, `angle_between`, Euclidean operations, lengths | Cairo documents measured ULP bounds or exact raw-integer behavior instead of f32's platform operations. Sections 2/2.2 cover the mechanism but not the public promise that these results intentionally differ. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Derived formatting and missing `Display`/`Deref` | all value types | Cairo derives `Debug`; glam has custom formatting and some array views. This is presentation/API rather than numeric semantics. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Extension-trait ownership | Euler and glamx matrix methods | Cairo requires importing a trait where Rust uses inherent methods or a different extension trait. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Public fixed helper handles | `fixed::wide::{Norm,Recip}` methods | These are public gas-saving handles, not glam APIs, and must be consumed through named methods. | **keep + add to DESIGN** |
| Camera/glam quirks intentionally retained | Vulkan orthographic Y translation and LH off-axis frustum terms | The Cairo item docs label upstream-identical behavior as a deviation. | **document better** (move to notes) |
| Performance-only prose in deviation sections | camera inlining, independent scalar calls, alternative benchmark references | These do not change behavior and obscure semantic auditing. | **document better** (move to implementation notes) |
| “Exact”, pass-by-value, and “as X” bullets | repeated throughout matrices, vectors, poses, swizzles | These are either parity statements or Cairo calling convention, not a difference in result. | **document better** |
| Stale camera module-wide omissions | `camera.cairo` | Lines 40-42 say Affine3/Quat view functions are not ported, but all eight exist in `camera/{lh,rh}/view.cairo:71-152`. | **bug** |

## Mandatory deep check: `Rot2::lerp`

For unit rotations at different angles, upstream `lerp` normally has length below one. At the
halfway point between `(1,0)` and `(0,1)`, upstream returns `(0.5,0.5)` (length `sqrt(0.5)`), while
Cairo returns approximately `(sqrt(0.5),sqrt(0.5))`. At the midpoint of antipodes `(1,0)` and
`(-1,0)`, upstream returns zero; Cairo's near-zero normalization returns `IDENTITY`. These are
observable changes, not merely different rounding.

A rapier/parry caller ported line by line therefore receives nlerp wherever it asked for a raw
linear blend. There is no `Rot2::nlerp` in glamx 0.3.1. The upstream alternatives are explicit:
`slerp` composes with an angle-scaled rotation (`glamx/src/rot2.rs:205-214`), and a caller wanting
nlerp can spell `lerp(...).normalize()`. Cairo's `Pose2::lerp` is unaffected: both upstream and
Cairo call `rotation.slerp` and linearly blend only translation (`glamx/src/pose2.rs:147-155`;
`packages/glamx/src/pose2.cairo:375-379`).

Recommendation: align `Rot2::lerp` with upstream before v0.1.0. Keeping normalization would violate
the repository's same-name rule and silently change line-by-line physics ports. If a normalized
linear interpolation is useful, add a separately named `nlerp` only after deciding whether that
API belongs in glamx.

## Threshold audit

One raw ULP is `2^-32 = 2.3283064365386963e-10`. “Sibling” compares call sites that should share a
policy, not unrelated thresholds with different conditioning.

| Item(s) | Upstream threshold | Cairo raw ULP / real value | Derivation in item/const docs? | Sibling consistency / finding |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Vec2/3/4::is_normalized`, `Quat::is_normalized` | `abs(len²-1) <= 2e-4` | 1,024 / `2.384185791015625e-7` | yes | all four identical |
| `Rot2::is_normalized` | `< 2e-4` | **missing API** | no | inconsistent with every vector/Quat type; bug |
| `Rot2::{normalize,from_mat}` near zero | `len > Real::EPSILON` | 1 / `2.328306436538696e-10` (identity at floored length <= 1) | yes | unique Rot2 policy; justified by representable resolution |
| `Vec2/3/4::move_towards` snap | `len <= 1e-4` | 0 / `0` | partial (“division is exact enough”), not an error-bound derivation | siblings identical; semantic change should be central |
| `Vec3::slerp` near parallel/antiparallel | dot band `1 - 3e-7` | gap 4,096; threshold raw 4,294,963,200 / `0.999999046325684` | yes | same raw value as Quat, with vector-angle derivation |
| `Quat::from_rotation_arc*` | `1 - 2*f32::EPSILON` | gap 4,096 / `9.5367431640625e-7`; threshold `0.999999046325684` | yes | same as Vec3/Quat slerp; intentionally wider than upstream |
| `Quat::{slerp,slerp_long}` | `1 - f32::EPSILON` | gap 4,096 / `9.5367431640625e-7`; threshold `0.999999046325684` | yes | both use the same helper; consistent |
| `Quat::{to_axis_angle,to_scaled_axis}` | vector length `1e-8` | 65,536 / `1.52587890625e-5` | yes | unique and justified by `1 ULP / length` |
| `Quat::is_near_identity` | `abs(w) > 1 - 1e-6` | gap 4,295; threshold raw 4,294,963,001 / `0.999998999992386` | yes | direct nearest quantization |
| `Quat::rotate_towards` | angle `<= 1e-4` | 429,497 / `0.000100000062957406` | yes | direct nearest quantization |
| Euler `to_euler` gimbal branch | `16*Real::EPSILON` | 16 / `3.725290298461914e-9` | yes, detailed conditioning discussion | all 12 Euler branches share `GIMBAL_EPS` |
| `Mat2/3/4::{inverse,try_inverse,inverse_or_zero}` | determinant `== 0` (or reciprocal non-finite) | 0 / `0` raw determinant | yes | all three consistent with DESIGN |
| `Affine2/3::inverse`, `SdpMatrix2/3::inverse` | determinant `== 0` | 0 / `0` | yes | consistent singularity rule |
| `Affine2::to_scale_angle_translation` | `det != 0` and each scale != 0 (assert) | no check; effective threshold none | item says unchecked, no consequence derivation | inconsistent with 3D; returns degenerate result |
| `Affine3`/`Mat4::to_scale_rotation_translation` | same exact-zero assertions | zero column triggers division at 0 / `0` | yes | determinant itself is unchecked; zero column panics |
| camera perspective/frustum preconditions | upstream optional `near > 0`, `far > 0`; no fov/aspect check | boundaries 0; PI raw 13,493,037,705 / `3.14159265370108`; aspect != 0 | conditions documented, derivation not applicable | LH/RH and GL/Vulkan/DX consistent; stronger than upstream |
| camera orthographic/frustum extents | nonzero denominators through IEEE division | exact zero difference / `0` | implicit in division docs | all projection siblings consistent |
| `SymmetricEigen3` stopping | closed-form eigenvector fallback `d_max < 1e-20` | scaled off-diagonal exactly 0 raw, otherwise cap at 6 sweeps | yes, measured corpus | different algorithm; no sibling |
| `any_orthonormal_vector/pair` | no epsilon; `signum(0)=+1` | no threshold | yes | both call sites share the DESIGN sign rule |

No unexplained sibling mismatch was found among the implemented threshold constants. The missing
Rot2 predicate and the 2D/3D affine degeneracy split are the two threshold-related defects.

## Suspected undocumented deviations

An item saying `Deviations: None.` is not safe merely because its `Panics` section is accurate.
There are 82 such items with a non-`Never` panic section (81 after excluding `Quat::abs_diff_eq`,
whose prose says “Never”). Representative behavior differences follow.

| Item(s) and input | Upstream result | Cairo result | Evidence / verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Fixed::from_ratio(1,0)` | float-style division is `+inf` | `'Fixed: division by zero'` | `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:113-121`; **document deviation** |
| `Fixed::{abs,copysign}(MIN)` | finite `+2^31` in f32 | overflow panic because `+2^31` is outside Q32.32 | `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:146-170`; **document deviation** |
| `Fixed::ceil(MAX)`; `Fixed::round(2^31-0.5)` and component-wise Vec variants | finite `2^31` in f32 | `'Fixed: overflow'` | `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:236-251`, vector docs e.g. `packages/glam/src/vec3.cairo:456-482`; **document deviation** |
| `Fixed::mul_add` with an out-of-range result | f32 produces a finite larger value or infinity | panic | `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:311-318`; **document overflow deviation** |
| `Fixed::remap` with equal input endpoints | NaN/infinity | delegated division-by-zero panic | `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:341-360`; **document deviation instead of `None`** |
| `Fixed::move_towards`, Pose append-translation, SDP addition, Rot2 inverse/to-matrix at representable extremes | f32/f64 arithmetic continues or reaches infinity | native/Fixed overflow panic | docs at `packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:389-398`, `packages/glamx/src/pose2.cairo:126-135`, `packages/glamx/src/rot2.cairo:105-152`, `packages/glamx/src/sdp.cairo:270-290`; **document overflow deviation** |
| `Mat2/3/4::abs` with a `Fixed::MIN` element | finite absolute value | overflow panic | `packages/glam/src/mat2.cairo:320-324` and siblings; **document deviation** |
| `fixed::wide` dot/norm/distance functions with out-of-range final value | no direct upstream API; float analog yields infinity | panic | e.g. `packages/fixed/src/wide.cairo:68-155`; `None` is misleading for a public fixed kernel; **document contract** |

The requested rounding-sensitive sample otherwise agrees with its docs:

- `round` is half away from zero; `fract(-1.25) = -0.25`; `rem_euclid` is nonnegative;
  `div_euclid` and remainder are exact on raw integers. The missing issue is overflow documentation,
  not the ordinary result (`packages/fixed/src/fixed.cairo:244-310`).
- Negative-base `powf` accepts integer exponents and rejects fractional ones, explicitly documented
  at `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:994-1012`.
- `atan2(0,0)=0`, `signum(0)=+1`, reversed `clamp`, and all `clamp_length*` preconditions are
  explicitly documented (`packages/fixed/src/trig.cairo:340-351`; `fixed.cairo:155-162`; generated
  Vec docs around `packages/glam/src/vec3.cairo:1180-1220`).

For `glam_assert!`, the sampled scalar Vec/Mat/Quat/Affine sources contain 91 upstream assertion
sites. Cairo item docs mention `glam_assert` 117 times and usually state the unchecked precondition.
The substantive exceptions are camera, where checks are mandatory rather than omitted, and affine
decomposition, where the consequences differ by dimension. Those are listed above. This audit did
not find a silently unchecked implemented precondition in the mandatory sample, but the proposed
machine-readable panic/precondition manifest below should replace grep-based confidence.

## Panic messages and tests

The inventory contains 61 distinct quoted panic strings and 839 occurrences. Every one of the 61
strings appears in at least one exact `#[should_panic(expected: ...)]` test. All custom library
messages are at most 31 characters. The custom messages consistently use `Type: reason`, including
the four `camera: ...` messages. Native corelib messages (`Division by 0`, `i64_add Overflow`,
`attempt to divide with overflow`, and siblings) do not follow that convention; changing them
would require replacing native operators and is therefore an API/gas decision, not a doc edit.

The inverse direction is not clean:

- Nine vector index messages occur in tests and implementation docs, but their public `IndexView`
  impl doc blocks have no `Panics`/`Deviations` template, so they are absent from this inventory.
- Three expected `'yx'` panics are test-helper self-tests in `test_swizzles.cairo`, not library
  panic paths.
- More importantly, 634 panic tests cannot cover 839 documented literal occurrences one-for-one.
  Reuse of a message across methods makes a string-set comparison pass while item-level coverage
  remains missing. This is a **release-blocking test audit**, not proof that 205 particular paths
  are missing: inherited “As method” docs and shared helper paths need an explicit mapping before
  the exact deficit can be assigned.

Recommendation: extend `scripts/deviations.py` or add a separate checker with an allowlisted
`(owner,item,message) -> test` manifest. Require every public panic item and every distinct branch
within it to map to one test; require every library `should_panic` test to map back. Also add the
full doc template to the nine `IndexView` impls.

## Proposed DESIGN section 3 wording

The following rows are ready to add after owner review:

| topic | glam-rs / glamx | glam.cairo proposed wording |
|---|---|---|
| algebraic reassociation | rounds after each f32 operation | products are accumulated by `fixed::wide` and rescaled once per output; equivalent formulas may intentionally differ bitwise from a line-by-line float port |
| interpolation | vector lerp uses `a*(1-t)+b*t`; glamx Rot2 lerp is unnormalized | fixed/vector lerp uses `a+(b-a)*t` for exact endpoints; same-name rotation lerps remain upstream-compatible (normalization requires an explicit `normalize`/`nlerp`) |
| small-distance snap | Vec `move_towards` snaps below `1e-4` | only an exactly zero representable distance is treated as zero; every nonzero Q32.32 distance is resolved |
| camera validation | `glam_assert!` checks positive near/far; other invalid inputs flow to IEEE values | camera projection constructors always check positive near/far, `0 < fov < PI`, nonzero aspect, and nonzero denominators; they panic with documented messages |
| integer overflow | depends on Rust debug/release and operation order | plain integer-vector operators panic; fused helpers range-check the final result only, so an out-of-range intermediate is accepted when the result fits |
| alternative algorithms | platform libm and glamx closed forms | deterministic fixed transcendentals and the scaled Jacobi eigen solver are public numeric choices; measured error bounds live on the items |
| API surface differences | inherent/trait/reference/iterator APIs | Cairo may use extension traits, named heterogeneous operations, pass-by-value, and omit borrow/iterator/display glue; every omission or addition is listed on the type |
| assertions and degeneracy | feature-gated preconditions | unchecked preconditions have unspecified out-of-contract results; checks retained as fixed safety rules (camera and singular division) are listed explicitly |

Do not add a row legitimizing normalized `Rot2::lerp`; that method should be corrected instead.

## Generated inventory

The appendix is generated from committed source. Run `python3 scripts/deviations.py --check` after
any doc change.

<!-- deviations-inventory:start -->
| Source | Owner | Kind | Item | Mirrors | Panics | Deviations |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:922` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `exp` | `f32::exp`. | * `'Fixed: exp overflow'` if the result does not fit the scalar range, i.e. for `self >= 21.487` (`31 ln 2`), where `f32::exp` returns a large value or infinity. | * Returns `ZERO` below `-22.873` (`-33 ln 2`), where the result is below half an ULP. * Rounds toward negative infinity: within 2.02 ULP below the exact value for results below `2^16`, within `7.2e-6 * 2^-30` relative above (see the table above). `exp(0) = 1` exactly, and the function is non-decreasing over the whole range. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:933` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `exp2` | `f32::exp2`. | * `'Fixed: exp overflow'` if `self >= 31`. | * Returns `ZERO` below `-33`, where the result is below half an ULP. * Rounds toward negative infinity: within 2.02 ULP below the exact value for results below `2^16`, within `7.2e-6 * 2^-30` relative above. `exp2(k) = 2^k` exactly for every integer `k` in `[-32, 30]`, and the function is non-decreasing. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:943` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `exp_m1` | `f32::exp_m1`. | * `'Fixed: exp overflow'` as [`ExpTrait::exp`]. | * Computed as `exp(self) - 1`: a fixed-point result has an absolute precision, so the cancellation that `f32::exp_m1` avoids near zero does not exist here (2.01 ULP on `[-1, 1]`). Returns `-1` below `-22.873`. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:952` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `ln` | `f32::ln`. | * `'Fixed: ln domain'` if `self <= 0` (`f32::ln` returns NaN or negative infinity). | * Maximum absolute error 0.66 ULP over the whole positive range; `ln(1) = 0` exactly and the function is non-decreasing. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:961` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `log2` | `f32::log2`. | * `'Fixed: ln domain'` if `self <= 0`. | * Maximum absolute error 0.75 ULP over the whole positive range; `log2(2^k) = k` exactly for every `k` in `[-32, 30]` and the function is non-decreasing. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:969` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `log10` | `f32::log10`. | * `'Fixed: ln domain'` if `self <= 0`. | * Maximum absolute error 0.57 ULP over the whole positive range; `log10(1) = 0` exactly. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:979` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `ln_1p` | `f32::ln_1p`. | * `'Fixed: ln domain'` if `self <= -1`. * `'i64_add Overflow'` if `self + 1` does not fit the scalar range. | * Computed as `ln(self + 1)`, which is exact in fixed point (the addition does not round): 0.64 ULP on `[-1/2, 1]`. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:993` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `log` | `f32::log`. | * `'Fixed: ln domain'` if `self <= 0` or `base <= 0`. * `'Fixed: division by zero'` if `base == 1`. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if the quotient does not fit the scalar range (`base` within `2^-31`-ish of 1). | * The division truncates, like every `/` of the crate: 1.88 ULP for the bases 2, 10, 1.5, 1/3 and 1000. A base close to 1 amplifies the error of the numerator by `1 / \|log2(base)\|`. |
| `packages/fixed/src/exp.cairo:1012` | `Fixed` | `fn` | `powf` | `f32::powf`. | * `'Fixed: overflow'` if the result does not fit the scalar range. * `'Fixed: division by zero'` if `self == 0` and `n < 0` (Rust returns infinity). * `'Fixed: powf domain'` if `self < 0` and `n` is not an integer (Rust returns NaN). * `'i64_neg Underflow'` if `self` is `MIN` (its magnitude does not fit). | * `0^n = 0` for `n > 0` and `x^0 = 1` for every `x`, as in Rust. * A negative base is accepted for an integer `n` only, and computed as `(-1)^n * \|self\|^n` (the same values as a positive base, with the sign of `powi`). * Error: the relative error of the `log2` stage (~`2^-36`) is multiplied by `\|n log(self)\|`, then `exp2` adds its own; measured for `self` in `[2^-8, 2^8]` and `n` in `[-4, 4]`: within `0.44 * 2^-30` relative for results above 1, within 2.05 ULP below. `powf(x, 1)` is therefore not bit-identical to `x`; use `powi` for integer exponents. * Returns `ZERO` when the exact result is below `2^-33`. |

The depth terms fit while `far / (far - near)` and `near * far / (far - near)` are below `2^30`, e.g. for `far >= 2 * near` and `far < 2^29`. `near * far` itself is never formed as a `Fixed`. | * `cot(vertical_fov / 2)` is one `sin_cos` (`fixed::trig`, 1.02 ULP) and one truncated division: `yy` is within `4 (1 + yy)^2` ULP of the exact value (the floored half angle, the two `sin_cos` errors and the division), `xx = yy / aspect_ratio` within that over `\|aspect_ratio\|` plus 1 ULP. * One division `q = far / (far - near)` (truncated) for both depth terms: `zz = +-(2 q - 1)` is within 2 ULP of the exact value and `tz = -2 near q` (one fused product, one floor rescale) within `2 near + 1` ULP. * `near > far` (a reversed depth range) is accepted, as in glam-rs. * `#[inline(always)]`: measured about 4k gas cheaper than a call (`alt_perspective_call` in `gas/camera.snap`). |
| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/proj.cairo:79` | `camera::lh::proj` | `fn` | `orthographic` | `glam::camera::lh::proj::opengl::orthographic`. | * `'Fixed: division by zero'` if `left == right`, `bottom == top` or `near == far`. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if a difference or a sum of the box (`right - left`, `top - bottom`, `far - near`, `left + right`, `bottom + top`, `far + near`) or an element does not fit the scalar range. | * Every quotient is rounded to nearest (one shared `Recip` per axis, and one for the depth) where glam-rs multiplies by a rounded reciprocal: each element is within 1 ULP of the exact value. * `#[inline(always)]`: measured about 4k gas cheaper than a call (`alt_orthographic_call` in `gas/camera.snap`). |
| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/proj.cairo:117` | `camera::lh::proj` | `fn` | `frustum` | `glam::camera::lh::proj::opengl::frustum`. | * `'camera: near not positive'` if `near <= 0` (the `glam_assert!` of glam-rs, checked here). * `'camera: far not positive'` if `far <= 0` (the `glam_assert!` of glam-rs, checked here). * `'Fixed: division by zero'` if `left == right`, `bottom == top` or `near == far`. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if `2 * near`, a difference or a sum of the box (`right - left`, `right + left`, `top - bottom`, `top + bottom`), a quotient or a depth term does not fit the scalar range. The depth terms fit while `far / (far - near)` and `near * far / (far - near)` are below `2^30`, as in `perspective`. | * `xx`, `yy`, `zx` and `zy` are quotients rounded to nearest (one shared `Recip` per axis): each is within 1 ULP of the exact value. * The depth terms are those of `perspective`: one truncated division and one fused product. `zz` is within 2 ULP and `tz` within `2 near + 1` ULP. * `near > far` is accepted, as in glam-rs. * As in glam-rs, the off-axis terms are `zx = (right + left) / (right - left)` and `zy = (top + bottom) / (top - bottom)` in the left-handed matrices too (same sign as the right-handed ones, with `w = +z`): an off-center left-handed frustum is mirrored, and the box maps to the NDC cube only when `right = -left` and `top = -bottom`. * `#[inline(always)]`: as `orthographic` (about 4k gas cheaper than a call). |
| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/proj.cairo:170` | `camera::lh::proj` | `fn` | `perspective` | `glam::camera::lh::proj::vulkan::perspective`. | * `'camera: fov out of range'` if `vertical_fov` is not in `(0, PI)` (glam-rs does not check it). * `'camera: aspect zero'` if `aspect_ratio` is zero. * `'camera: near not positive'` if `near <= 0` (the `glam_assert!` of glam-rs, checked here). * `'camera: far not positive'` if `far <= 0` (the `glam_assert!` of glam-rs, checked here). * `'Fixed: division by zero'` if `vertical_fov` is one raw ULP (the half angle floors to zero) or if `near == far`. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if `cot(vertical_fov / 2)` (`vertical_fov` below about `1e-9`), `xx` or a depth term does not fit the scalar range. The depth terms fit while `far / (far - near)` and `near * far / (far - near)` are below `2^31`, e.g. for `far >= 2 * near` and `far < 2^31`. `near * far` itself is never formed as a `Fixed`. | * `cot(vertical_fov / 2)` is one `sin_cos` (`fixed::trig`, 1.02 ULP) and one truncated division: `yy` is within `4 (1 + yy)^2` ULP of the exact value (the floored half angle, the two `sin_cos` errors and the division), `xx = yy / aspect_ratio` within that over `\|aspect_ratio\|` plus 1 ULP. * One division `q = far / (far - near)` (truncated) for both depth terms: `zz = +-q` is within 1 ULP of the exact value and `tz = -near * q` (one fused product, one floor rescale) within `near + 1` ULP. * `near > far` (a reversed depth range) is accepted, as in glam-rs. * `#[inline(always)]`: measured about 4k gas cheaper than a call (`alt_perspective_call` in `gas/camera.snap`). |

| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/view.cairo:37` | `camera::lh::view` | `fn` | `look_at_mat4` | `glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4`. | * `'Vec3: normalize zero'` if `center == eye`, or if the direction and `up` are parallel. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if an element does not fit the scalar range. * `'i64_neg Underflow'` if an element is `MIN`. * `'i64_sub Overflow'` / `'i64_sub Underflow'` if an element difference leaves the scalar range. | * The `glam_assert!` preconditions are not checked (docs/DESIGN.md section 3): `up` must be normalized. * Bit-identical to `Mat4::look_at_lh` (`glam::mat4`), inlined: measured 4.2k (`look_to`) to 5.1k (`look_at`) gas cheaper than calling it (`alt_look_*_mat4_delegate` in `gas/camera.snap`). `s` is normalized (one square root, one shared division), every other element is exact or one floored `dot3`. |
| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/view.cairo:58` | `camera::lh::view` | `fn` | `look_to_mat4` | `glam::camera::lh::view::look_to_mat4`. | * `'Vec3: normalize zero'` if `dir` and `up` are parallel. * `'Fixed: overflow'` if an element does not fit the scalar range. * `'i64_neg Underflow'` if an element is `MIN`. | * The `glam_assert!` preconditions are not checked (docs/DESIGN.md section 3): `dir` and `up` must be normalized. * Bit-identical to `Mat4::look_to_lh` (`glam::mat4`), inlined: measured 4.2k (`look_to`) to 5.1k (`look_at`) gas cheaper than calling it (`alt_look_*_mat4_delegate` in `gas/camera.snap`). `s` is normalized (one square root, one shared division), every other element is exact or one floored `dot3`. |

The shared right-handed Mat4 look-to kernel is used with the normalized direction negated, then its affine columns are extracted. |
| `packages/glam/src/camera/lh/view.cairo:86` | `camera::lh::view` | `fn` | `look_to_affine3` | `glam::camera::lh::view::look_to_affine3`. | * As [`look_to_mat4`]. | * The `glam_assert!` preconditions are not checked. | `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:362` | `Mat3Euler` | `fn` | `from_euler` | `glam::Mat3::from_euler`. | * Never (every entry is a product of sines and cosines, so `\|entry\| <= 2`). | * A method of an extension trait, not of `Mat3Trait`: `use glam::euler::Mat3EulerTrait;` brings it into scope. * Each entry is a single rescale of the exact sum of two triple products instead of the two chained roundings of glam-rs: the result is the floor of the exact entry, within 3 ULP of the real one (1 ULP per `sin_cos`, amplified by factors of modulus <= 1). |
| `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:381` | `Mat3Euler` | `fn` | `to_euler` | `glam::Mat3::to_euler`. | * `'Fixed: overflow'` if `sqrt(a^2 + b^2)` of two entries of `self` leaves the scalar range, which no rotation matrix does (`\|entry\| <= 1`). | * A method of an extension trait, not of `Mat3Trait`. * The gimbal-lock threshold is [`GIMBAL_EPS`] (16 raw ULP), the Q32.32 transposition of the `16 * EPSILON` of glam-rs. * The error is that of [`fixed::trig::TrigTrait::atan2`] (3.22 ULP) plus the 1 ULP of the floored `sqrt`, as long as the rotation is not close to gimbal lock; see [`GIMBAL_EPS`] for the conditioning near it. |
| `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:471` | `Mat4Euler` | `fn` | `from_euler` | `glam::Mat4::from_euler`. | * Never. | * A method of an extension trait, not of `Mat4Trait`. * Same rounding as [`Mat3EulerTrait::from_euler`], whose result it embeds. |
| `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:484` | `Mat4Euler` | `fn` | `to_euler` | `glam::Mat4::to_euler`. | * `'Fixed: overflow'`, as [`Mat3EulerTrait::to_euler`]. | * A method of an extension trait, not of `Mat4Trait`. * Same values as [`Mat3EulerTrait::to_euler`] of `Mat3::from_mat4(self)`. |
| `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:513` | `QuatEuler` | `fn` | `from_euler` | `glam::Quat::from_euler`. | * Never (every component is a product of sines and cosines of half angles). | * A method of an extension trait, not of `QuatTrait`: `use glam::euler::QuatEulerTrait;` brings it into scope. * Each component is a single rescale of the exact sum of two triple products instead of the two chained roundings of glam-rs: the result is the floor of the exact component, within 3 ULP of the real one. * The result is a unit quaternion up to that rounding; it is not renormalized, exactly as in glam-rs. |
| `packages/glam/src/euler.cairo:531` | `QuatEuler` | `fn` | `to_euler` | `glam::Quat::to_euler`. | * `'i64_add Overflow'` / `'i64_add Underflow'` if a component of `self` is outside `[-2^30, 2^30)`: the rotation matrix doubles every component first. A normalized quaternion never reaches it. | * A method of an extension trait, not of `QuatTrait`. * Goes through the rotation matrix of `self`, as glam-rs does. The nine entries are fused (one rescale each) instead of glam-rs' twelve rounded products. * The error is that of [`Mat3EulerTrait::to_euler`] (5 ULP) plus the 2 ULP of the matrix entries divided by the residual of the order (see [`GIMBAL_EPS`]). |
